(herald "Verification of TLS 1.3 rules to assist in mTLS verification"
  (try-old-strands))

(comment "All input read from F3_agreement_no_axiom.scm")
(comment "Old strands tried first")

(defprotocol mtls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt)
      (spk cpk akey) (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defrule client-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((x rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 1)) (p "" "x" z-0 x)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule server-exponent-unavailable
    (forall ((y rndx) (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 2)) (p "" "x" z-0 y)
          (p "server" "y" z y) (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-1
    (forall
      ((cr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "client" z (idx 2))
          (p "client" "spk" z spk) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul x y)))
          (p "client" "y" z y) (p "client" "x" z x)
          (p "client" "cr" z cr) (non (invk spk)) (uniq-at cr z (idx 0))
          (ugen x))
        (false))))
  (defrule diffie-hellman-value-between-legitimate-parties-unavailable-2
    (forall
      ((sr random32) (cpk akey) (c ca name) (y rndx) (x expt)
        (z z-0 strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "" z-0 (idx 2)) (p "server" z (idx 3))
          (p "server" "cpk" z cpk) (p "server" "ca" z ca)
          (p "server" "c" z c) (p "" "x" z-0 (exp (gen) (mul y x)))
          (p "server" "y" z y) (p "server" "x" z x)
          (p "server" "sr" z sr) (non (invk cpk)) (uniq-at sr z (idx 1))
          (ugen y))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
            (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "y" z-0 y))))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (cat c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                (enc
                  (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
                (hash
                  (hash "finished"
                    (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                  (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                  (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                c cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))))
  (label 0)
  (unrealized (0 1))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
        (y y) (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0)))
  (comment "1 in cohort - 1 not yet seen"))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk cpk akey)
    (s c ca name) (x y rndx))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x))
  (precedes ((0 0) (1 0)) ((1 1) (0 1)))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "cpk" z-0 cpk)
            (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "y" z-0 y))))))
  (operation encryption-test (added-strand server 2)
    (enc
      (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
        (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)) (0 1))
  (strand-map 0)
  (traces

      (recv

          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s

      (send

          (enc
            (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
              (hash
                (hash "finished"
                  (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
                (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

                    spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c
              cpk (enc (hash c cpk) (privk ca))) (invk cpk))
          (hash

            (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response request-0 text)
    (cpk spk cpk-0 akey) (c s c-0 ca name) (x y rndx))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
    (y y))
  (defstrand server 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request-0)
    (response response) (spk spk) (cpk cpk-0) (s s) (c c-0) (ca ca)
    (y y) (x x))
  (precedes ((0 0) (1 0)) ((1 1) (0 1)) ((1 4) (0 4)))
  (non-orig (invk spk))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "cpk" z cpk)
          (p "client" "c" z c) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (uniq-at cr z 0)
          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)

(defskeleton mtls13
